## Psalms 83:7 (id: 10019083007)

7mo ol man blong Gebal, mo ol man Amon, mo ol laen blong Amalek, mo ol man Filistia, mo ol man Taea.


## Psalms 83:8 (id: 10019083008)

8Mo ol man Asiria tu oli joen wetem olgeta, from we oli strong man blong faet, mo oli gudfala fren blong ol man Amon mo ol man Moab we oli laen blong Lot.


## Psalms 83:9 (id: 10019083009)

9Plis yu mekem long olgeta olsem we yu bin mekem long ol man Midian bifo, olsem we yu bin mekem long tufala ya Siseraha mo Jabin, long Kison Reva.


## Psalms 83:10 (id: 10019083010)

10Yu yu winim tufala long taon ya Endoro, mo bodi blong tufala i roten long ples ya.


## Psalms 83:11 (id: 10019083011)

11Plis yu mekem long ol lida blong olgeta olsem we yu bin mekem long tufala haeman ya Oreb mo Seheb. Plis yu winim ol king blong olgeta olsem we yu bin winim tufala king ya Seba mo Jalmuna,


## Psalms 83:12 (id: 10019083012)

12we tufala i talem se, “Graon ya we i blong God, bambae yumi tekem i blong yumi.”


## Psalms 83:13 (id: 10019083013)

13God, plis yu mekem olgeta oli go wanwan, olsem asis blong graon we win i blu long hem i go olbaot.


## Psalms 83:14 (id: 10019083014)

14Yu kam long olgeta olsem bigfala faea we i laet long bus, we i stap kakae ol tri olbaot long ol hil.


## Psalms 83:15 (id: 10019083015)

15Plis yu kam long olgeta olsem bigfala win, yu mekem olgeta oli fraet. Yu kam olsem bigfala hariken, yu ronemaot olgeta oli go.


## Psalms 83:16 (id: 10019083016)

16Hae God, yu mekem we olgeta oli sem gud, gogo oli lukaot rod blong kam man blong yu.


## Psalms 83:17 (id: 10019083017)

17Plis yu winimgud olgeta, yu mekem we bambae oli fraet gogo i no save finis, yu mekem olgeta oli ded long wan fasin we bambae i mekem nem blong olgeta i nogud.


## Psalms 83:18 (id: 10019083018)

18Plis yu mekem olgeta oli save we yu nomo yu Hae God, yu nao yu hae King blong olgeta ples long wol.


## Psalms 84:1 (id: 10019084001)

1Hae God. Yu yu gat olgeta paoa. !Mi mi laekem haos\* blong yu tumas!


## Psalms 84:2 (id: 10019084002)

2!Mi wantem tumas blong go stap long hem! Hae God, mi wantem tumas blong stap oltaem long haos blong yu. Yu yu God we yu laef. Tingting blong mi i glad tumas long yu, mo mi stap sing blong presem yu.


## Psalms 84:3 (id: 10019084003)

3Hae God, yu yu gat olgeta paoa. Yu yu King blong mi, yu yu God blong mi. Ol smosmol pijin ya we oli smol tumas, be oli stap mekem nes blong olgeta long haos blong yu, mo oli stap lukaot long ol pikinini blong olgeta klosap long olta blong yu.


## Psalms 84:4 (id: 10019084004)

4Ol man we oli stap long haos blong yu oli hapi tumas, oltaem oli stap singsing blong presem yu.


## Psalms 84:5 (id: 10019084005)

5Ol man we yu yu stap mekem olgeta oli strong, oli hapi tumas. Oltaem oli wantem tumas blong kam long hil ya Saeon\* blong mekem prea long yu.


## Psalms 84:6 (id: 10019084006)

6Oltaem, taem oli pastru long krik ya Baka we i drae we i drae, be oli no moa trabol blong kasem wota, from we ples ya i kam ples blong wota. Ren blong taem ya i mekem ol hol long ples ya oli kam fulap long wota.


## Psalms 84:7 (id: 10019084007)

7Yu yu God, yu yu hae moa long ol narafala god. Oltaem, taem ol man ya oli stap kam long Saeon oli stap kam strong moa, mo bambae oli stap luk yu long ples ya blong yu.


## Psalms 84:8 (id: 10019084008)

8Hae God, yu yu God blong mi, mo yu yu gat olgeta paoa. !Plis yu harem prea blong mi! Yu yu God blong Jekob.\* !Plis yu lesin long mi!


## Psalms 84:9 (id: 10019084009)

9God, yu nao yu jusumaot king blong mifala. !Plis yu blesem hem!


## Psalms 84:10 (id: 10019084010)

10Yu yu God blong mi. Sipos mi stap wan dei nomo long haos blong yu, be hemia i gud moa, i winim we mi stap wan taosen dei long ol narafala ples. Nating sipos mi stap wok nomo long doa blong haos blong yu, be hemia i moa gud, i winim we mi stap insaed olgeta long haos blong ol man nogud.


## Psalms 84:11 (id: 10019084011)

11Hae God, yu yu God blong mifala. Yu yu King we paoa blong yu i bigfala tumas, mo yu stap blokem mifala blong mifala i stap gud. Yu yu stap blesem mifala long gladhat blong yu, yu stap leftemap nem blong mifala. Yu yu no save holemtaet ol gudgudfala samting blong yu, oltaem yu stap givim long olgeta we oli stap mekem ol stret fasin.


## Psalms 84:12 (id: 10019084012)

12Hae God, yu yu gat olgeta paoa. Olgeta we oli stap trastem yu bambae oli save harem gud.


## Psalms 85:1 (id: 10019085001)

1Hae God. Kantri ya Isrel\* i kantri blong yu. Bifo, yu yu bin gat sore long hem, mo yu bin mekem hem i kam gud bakegen.


## Psalms 85:2 (id: 10019085002)

2Yu yu bin fogivim mifala yu tekemaot ol sin blong mifala, mo yu bin tekemaot olgeta rong blong mifala.


## Psalms 85:3 (id: 10019085003)

3Long taem ya, yu bin lego fasin ya we yu stap kros long mifala, mo yu bin livim kros blong yu, yu no moa kros long mifala bakegen.


## Psalms 85:4 (id: 10019085004)

4God, oltaem yu yu stap sevem mifala. Plis yu tekem mifala i kambak long yu bakegen, yu no moa tanem baksaed long mifala.


## Psalms 85:5 (id: 10019085005)

5?Olsem wanem? ?Bambae yu stap kros long mifala oltaem nomo? ?Bambae kros blong yu i no save finis samtaem?


## Psalms 85:6 (id: 10019085006)

6Plis yu mekem mifala i strong bakegen, nao bambae mifala i presem yu.


## Psalms 85:7 (id: 10019085007)

7Hae God, oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas. Plis yu soemaot fasin ya long mifala, yu halpem mifala, yu sevem mifala.


## Psalms 85:8 (id: 10019085008)

8Hae God i God blong yumi, mo mi mi stap lesin long ol tok ya we hem i stap talem. Hem i promes se sipos yumi no gobak long ol krangke fasin blong yumi, hem bambae i givim pis long yumi.


## Psalms 85:9 (id: 10019085009)

9Tru, oltaem nomo hem i rere blong sevem man we i stap ona long hem, mo hem bambae i stap long ples blong yumi long olgeta paoa blong hem, nao bambae hem i stap sevem yumi.


## Psalms 85:10 (id: 10019085010)

10Oltaem God bambae i stap lavem man tumas, nao man bambae i putum tingting blong hem i stap strong long God. God bambae i soemaot stret fasin blong hem, nao man bambae i kasem pis long hem.


## Psalms 85:11 (id: 10019085011)

11Fasin ya we man i stap strong long God bambae i go antap i kasem heven, mo stret fasin ya blong God bambae i kamdaon i kasem graon.


## Psalms 85:12 (id: 10019085012)

12Hae God bambae i mekem we yumi gat plante samting, mo graon blong yumi bambae i karem plante kakae.


## Psalms 85:13 (id: 10019085013)

13Stret fasin blong Hae God bambae i go fastaem long hem, mo bambae i mekemrere rod blong hem.


## Psalms 86:1 (id: 10019086001)

1Hae God. Mi mi no strong, mi no gat paoa. Plis yu harem krae blong mi, yu ansa long mi.


## Psalms 86:2 (id: 10019086002)

2Mi mi man blong wok blong yu, mi trastem yu. Oltaem tingting blong mi i stap strong long yu. Plis yu sevem mi, yu no letem mi mi ded.


## Psalms 86:3 (id: 10019086003)

3Yu yu God blong mi, plis yu sore long mi. Oltaem, mi mi stap prea long yu, i stat long moning go kasem long sapa.


## Psalms 86:4 (id: 10019086004)

4Masta, mi mi man blong wok blong yu. Mi stap prea i go long yu nomo. Plis yu mekem mi mi glad.


## Psalms 86:5 (id: 10019086005)

5Yu yu gud tumas long mifala, mo yu stap fogivim mifala, yu stap tekemaot ol sin blong mifala. Ol man we oli stap mekem prea long yu, oltaem yu yu lavem olgeta tumas.


## Psalms 86:6 (id: 10019086006)

6Hae God, mi stap krae blong yu givhan long mi. Plis yu lesin long prea blong mi, mo yu ansa long mi.


## Psalms 86:7 (id: 10019086007)

7Oltaem, taem mi mi stap long trabol, mi stap singaot long yu, from we mi save se yu yu stap ansa long ol prea blong mi.


## Psalms 86:8 (id: 10019086008)

8Masta, i no gat narafala god we i olsem yu. I no gat wan i stap we i stap mekem ol gudgudfala wok olsem we yu yu bin mekem.


## Psalms 86:9 (id: 10019086009)

9Ol man blong olgeta kantri, yu yu bin mekem olgeta, mo bambae oli kam, oli bodaon long yu, oli presem hae nem blong yu.


## Psalms 86:10 (id: 10019086010)

10Yu nomo yu God. Yu yu gat paoa, mo yu stap mekem ol bigbigfala samting.


## Psalms 86:11 (id: 10019086011)

11Hae God, plis yu tijim mi long ol samting we yu wantem blong mi mi mekem, nao bambae mi obei long yu, mi holem trutok blong yu. Yu mekem we mi bambae mi wantem tumas blong mekem ol wok blong yu nomo.


## Psalms 86:12 (id: 10019086012)

12God, yu yu Masta\* blong mi. Bambae mi stap presem yu long olgeta tingting blong mi, bambae mi stap leftemap hae nem blong yu oltaem, gogo i no save finis.


## Psalms 86:13 (id: 10019086013)

13Oltaem yu yu lavem mi tumas. Fasin ya blong yu i gud we i gud. Yu yu bin sevem mi blong mi no go long beregraon.


## Psalms 86:14 (id: 10019086014)

14God, ol praod man oli kam oli agens long mi. Wan grup blong ol man nogud oli stap traem blong kilim mi. Olgeta ya, neva oli save lesin long yu.


## Psalms 86:15 (id: 10019086015)

15Be Masta, yu yu God. Fasin blong yu, oltaem yu yu gat sore long man, mo yu lavem hem tumas. Oltaem yu stap mekem tingting blong yu i longfala, mo yu yu kaen tumas, oltaem yu stap holem ol promes blong yu.


## Psalms 86:16 (id: 10019086016)

16Plis yu lukluk i kam long mi, yu sore long mi, yu sevem mi, mo yu mekem mi mi strong, from we mi mi stap wok blong yu, olsem we mama blong mi i stap mekem bifo.


## Psalms 86:17 (id: 10019086017)

17Hae God, plis yu soemaot long mi we fasin blong yu i gud tumas, nao taem olgeta we oli no laekem mi nating oli luk we yu yu stap givhan long mi, yu stap leftemap tingting blong mi, bambae olgeta oli sem.


## Psalms 87:1 (id: 10019087001)

1Taon ya Jerusalem,\* hem i taon blong Hae God. Hem i wokem long tabu hil\* ya Saeon,\*


## Psalms 87:2 (id: 10019087002)

2mo i laekem taon ya moa, i winim ol narafala taon long Isrel.\*


## Psalms 87:3 (id: 10019087003)

3Yufala long taon ya blong hem, yufala i lesin long ol gudgudfala tok we hem i stap talem blong leftemap taon ya.


## Psalms 87:4 (id: 10019087004)

4Hem i talem se, “Taem mi mekem lis blong olgeta man we oli stap obei long mi, bambae mi putum nem blong ol man Ijip wetem ol man Babilonia, mo ol man Filistia, mo ol man Taea, mo ol man Itiopia, olgeta evriwan oli stap insaed. Olgeta ya bambae mi kaontem wetem yufala, olsem we oli man ples blong yufala.”


## Psalms 87:5 (id: 10019087005)

5Mo bakegen, hem bambae i talem se, “Taon ya, ol man blong olgeta kantri bambae oli gat raet long hem.” Hae God, hem i hae we i hae, mo hem bambae i mekem taon ya i strong.


## Psalms 87:6 (id: 10019087006)

6Hem bambae i raetemdaon nem blong olgeta long wan buk, mo bambae hem i kaontem olgeta evriwan, se oli man Jerusalem.


## Psalms 87:7 (id: 10019087007)

7Nao olgeta ya bambae oli stap danis, mo oli stap singsing se, “Saeon\* ya, olgeta gudgudfala samting blong yumi oli stap kamaot long hem oltaem.”


## Psalms 88:1 (id: 10019088001)

1Hae God. Yu yu God blong mi, mo oltaem yu stap sevem mi. Long dei mo long naet, mi stap krae long yu, blong yu yu halpem mi.


## Psalms 88:2 (id: 10019088002)

2Plis yu harem prea blong mi. Mi mi stap krae long yu blong yu givhan long mi. Plis yu lesin long mi.


## Psalms 88:3 (id: 10019088003)

3Plante trabol tumas oli stap kasem mi, we klosap mi mi ded from.


## Psalms 88:4 (id: 10019088004)

4Naoia, paoa blong mi i lus olgeta, oli stap kaontem mi wetem olgeta we klosap oli ded.


## Psalms 88:5 (id: 10019088005)

5Ol man oli luk mi olsem we mi mi ded finis. Mi mi olsem ol man we oli ded long faet, we oli stap finis long beregraon. Yu yu fogetem olgeta finis, yu no moa save givhan long olgeta.


## Psalms 88:6 (id: 10019088006)

6Yu sakem mi finis insaed long hol we i godaon we i godaon olgeta. Hol ya i godaon we i godaon, mo i tudak tu.


## Psalms 88:7 (id: 10019088007)

7Yu yu kros tumas long mi, Kros blong yu i flatem mi olgeta, i olsem we bigsi i rolem mi, i flatemgud mi.


## Psalms 88:8 (id: 10019088008)

8Yu yu mekem we ol fren blong mi oli lego mi finis, yu mekem mi mi kam olsem wan samting we i nogud tumas long fes blong olgeta. Yu blokemgud ol rod blong mi, nao mi mi no save ronwe.


## Psalms 88:9 (id: 10019088009)

9Mi mi harem nogud tumas, mi krae gogo ae blong mi i kam nogud from. Hae God. Evri dei nomo, mi mi stap singaot long yu, mi stap leftemap han blong mi, mi stap prea long yu.


## Psalms 88:10 (id: 10019088010)

10?Olsem wanem? ?Ol merikel we yu yu stap mekem, be ol dedman oli save luk? !Ol dedman oli no save girap bakegen blong presem yu!


## Psalms 88:11 (id: 10019088011)

11?Oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas, mo yu stap holem ol promes blong yu, be olgeta we oli stap finis long beregraon oli save talemaot ol fasin ya?


## Psalms 88:12 (id: 10019088012)

12Olgeta ya oli stap long ples ya we ol man oli fogetem olgeta finis, mo oli no save luk ol gudgudfala fasin blong yu. !Olgeta we oli stap finis long tudak ya, oli no save luk ol merikel ya blong yu!


## Psalms 88:13 (id: 10019088013)

13Hae God. Evri moning mi stap prea long yu, mi stap krae long yu blong yu givhan long mi.


## Psalms 88:14 (id: 10019088014)

14Hae God. ?From wanem yu tanem baksaed long mi? ?From wanem yu tanem fes blong yu i gowe long mi?


## Psalms 88:15 (id: 10019088015)

15I stat long taem we mi mi yangfala, kam kasem tede, oltaem nomo, mi mi stap harem nogud long bodi blong mi, we klosap mi ded from. Panis ya we yu stap givim long mi hem i hevi tumas, nao i mekem mi mi slak we mi slak.


## Psalms 88:16 (id: 10019088016)

16Yu yu kros tumas long mi nao tingting blong mi i foldaon. Yu mekem i strong tumas long mi, yu mekem mi mi kam nogud olgeta.


## Psalms 88:17 (id: 10019088017)

17Oltaem nomo, i stat long moning go kasem long sapa, panis ya we yu stap givim long mi i kam kavremap mi olsem wota we i ron bigwan, i hivap raon long mi long evri saed.


## Psalms 88:18 (id: 10019088018)

18Yu yu mekem olgeta man oli gowe long mi. Nating we oli gudfala fren blong mi, be yu mekem olgeta oli lego mi, naoia tudak nomo i fren blong mi.


## Psalms 89:1 (id: 10019089001)

1Hae God. Oltaem bambae mi stap singsing from fasin ya blong yu we oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas. Fasin ya blong yu we oltaem yu stap holem ol promes blong yu, bambae mi stap talemaot oltaem nomo, gogo i no save finis.


## Psalms 89:2 (id: 10019089002)

2Mi mi save we fasin ya blong yu blong lavem man bambae i stap oltaem nomo, mo fasin ya blong yu blong holem ol promes blong yu bambae i olsem skae, i no save lus.


## Psalms 89:3 (id: 10019089003)

3Yu yu talem se, “Mi mi mekem promes finis wetem Deved, man ya blong wok blong mi we mi mi jusumaot hem bifo.


## Psalms 89:4 (id: 10019089004)

4Mi mi talem long hem se, ‘Oltaem ol king blong ol man blong mi bambae oli stap kamaot long olgeta we oli stap kamaot biaen long yu. Olgeta ya, mi bambae mi holem olgeta oli stap gud oltaem, bambae mi stap lukaot gud long olgeta gogo i no save finis.’ ”


## Psalms 89:5 (id: 10019089005)

5Hae God. Olgeta long heven oli stap singsing blong talemaot ol gudgudfala fasin we yu yu mekem, oli stap sing from fasin ya we yu yu stap holem ol promes blong yu.


## Psalms 89:6 (id: 10019089006)

6I no gat man long heven we i olsem yu. Long olgeta we oli stap long heven, be i no gat wan man nating we i sem mak long yu.


## Psalms 89:7 (id: 10019089007)

7Olgeta enjel long heven oli tabu we i tabu, be oli stap ona long yu. Olgeta evriwan oli stap fraet long yu, from we yu yu hae we yu hae.


## Psalms 89:8 (id: 10019089008)

8Hae God, yu yu God blong mi, mo yu gat olgeta paoa. I no gat wan man nating we i strong olsem yu. Long olgeta samting, oltaem yu yu stap holem ol promes blong yu.


## Psalms 89:9 (id: 10019089009)

9Solwota i strong tumas, be yu yu stap rulum hem. Taem we hem i raf tumas, be yu yu stap mekem i kam kwaet bakegen.


## Psalms 89:10 (id: 10019089010)

10Yu yu flatemgud Rahab,\* bigfala anamol ya blong solwota, mo yu kilim hem i ded. Yu yu stap winim ol enemi blong yu long paoa blong yu we i strong tumas.


## Psalms 89:11 (id: 10019089011)

11Skae i blong yu, mo graon ya tu i blong yu. Yu yu mekem wol ya wetem olgeta samting we i stap long hem.


## Psalms 89:12 (id: 10019089012)

12Yu yu mekem olgeta ples, i stat long not, i go kasem long saot. Hil ya Taboro mo hil ya Hemon tufala i glad tumas, tufala i stap singsing blong presem yu.


## Psalms 89:13 (id: 10019089013)

13!Yu yu gat paoa blong mekem olgeta samting! !Paoa blong yu i bigwan tumas!


## Psalms 89:14 (id: 10019089014)

14Yu yu King, we fasin blong yu, oltaem yu stap mekem i stret, oltaem yu stap jajem ol man long stret rod. Olgeta samting we yu stap mekem oli stap soemaot we oltaem yu stap lavem man tumas, mo oltaem yu stap holem ol promes blong yu.


## Psalms 89:15 (id: 10019089015)

15Olgeta we oli stap singsing blong presem yu, oli glad tumas. Oltaem yu yu kaen gud long olgeta, nao i olsem we oli stap wokbaot long delaet.


## Psalms 89:16 (id: 10019089016)

16Yu yu mekem olgeta oli harem gud, i stat long moning go kasem long sapa. Mo oltaem oli stap presem yu from ol gudgudfala fasin blong yu.


## Psalms 89:17 (id: 10019089017)

17Oltaem yu yu mekem mifala i stap win gud long faet. Yu stap mekem mifala i win from we yu lavem mifala tumas.


## Psalms 89:18 (id: 10019089018)

18Hae God, yu yu tabu we yu tabu. Yu yu God blong mifala ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* mo yu jusumaot king ya blong mifala, we i stap lukaot long mifala, yu putumap hem i kam king.


## Psalms 89:19 (id: 10019089019)

19Longtaem bifo, yu yu kamtru long ol man blong wok blong yu, we tingting blong olgeta i stap strong long yu, yu toktok long olgeta long vison.\* Yu yu talem se, “Mi mi givhan long wan soldia we i gat hae nem. Mi jusumaot hem long olgeta man, nao mi putumap hem i kam king.


## Psalms 89:20 (id: 10019089020)

20Soldia ya Deved ya, we i man blong wok blong mi, mo mi kafsaedem tabu oel i go long hed blong hem, mi mekem hem i king.


## Psalms 89:21 (id: 10019089021)

21Mi bambae mi mekem hem i strong, oltaem paoa blong mi bambae i stap wetem hem.


## Psalms 89:22 (id: 10019089022)

22Ol enemi blong hem neva bambae oli winim hem, ol man nogud bambae oli no save daonem hem samtaem.


## Psalms 89:23 (id: 10019089023)

23Bambae mi flatem ol enemi blong hem, mi kilim olgeta evriwan we oli no laekem hem, oli stap agens long hem.


## Psalms 89:24 (id: 10019089024)

24Be hem, bambae mi lavem hem tumas, tingting blong mi bambae i stap strong long hem oltaem, mo bambae mi mekem we hem i stap win oltaem.


## Psalms 89:25 (id: 10019089025)

25Bambae mi mekem kingdom blong hem i kam bigwan, i stat long Mediterenean Si go kasem Yufretes Reva.


## Psalms 89:26 (id: 10019089026)

26Bambae hem i talem long mi se, ‘Yu yu papa blong mi, mo yu yu God blong mi. Oltaem yu stap lukaot gud long mi, yu stap sevem mi.’


## Psalms 89:27 (id: 10019089027)

27Bambae mi mekem hem i fasbon pikinini blong mi, mo bambae hem i hae we i hae, i winim ol narafala king.


## Psalms 89:28 (id: 10019089028)

28Oltaem bambae mi stap holem promes blong mi we mi mekem wetem hem, mo promes ya bambae i stap oltaem, i no save finis.


## Psalms 89:29 (id: 10019089029)

29Olgeta we bambae oli stap kamaot biaen long hem, bambae mi mi holem olgeta oli stap gud oltaem, olsem we skae i stap oltaem nomo. Oltaem ol king blong ol man blong mi bambae oli stap kamaot biaen long olgeta ya.


## Psalms 89:30-32 (id: 10019089030)

30\-32“Be sipos olgeta ya oli no obei long ol loa blong mi, mo oli no stap folem ol tok we mi stap givim long olgeta, bambae mi mi panisim olgeta from ol sin ya blong olgeta. ## Psalms 113:3 (id: 10019113003)

3Yumi evriwan, i stat long is, i go kasem long wes, yumi presem nem blong Hae God.


## Psalms 113:4 (id: 10019113004)

## Psalms 113:5 (id: 10019113005)

## Psalms 115:1 (id: 10019115001)

1Hae God. Oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas, mo oltaem yu stap holem ol promes blong yu. From samting ya, mifala i no save leftemap nem blong mifala, mifala i mas leftemap nem blong yu nomo.


## Psalms 115:2 (id: 10019115002)

2Olgeta. I no stret blong ol hiten man oli stap jikim yumi se, “?Wehem God ya blong yufala?”


## Psalms 115:3 (id: 10019115003)

## Psalms 115:4 (id: 10019115004)

## Psalms 115:5 (id: 10019115005)

5Ol god ya oli gat maot, be oli no save toktok. Oli gat ae, be oli no save lukluk.


## Psalms 115:6 (id: 10019115006)

## Psalms 115:7 (id: 10019115007)

## Psalms 115:8 (id: 10019115008)

8I gud ol man we oli mekem ol aedol ya, wetem ol man we oli stap wosipim olgeta, olgeta evriwan oli kam olsem ol aedol ya nomo.


## Psalms 115:9 (id: 10019115009)

9Yumi ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* yumi mas trastem Hae God. Oltaem hem i stap givhan long yumi, i stap lukaot gud long yumi,

10Yufala ol pris\* blong hem, yufala i mas trastem hem. Oltaem hem i stap givhan long yumi, i stap lukaot gud long yumi.

12Oltaem hem i stap tingbaot yumi, mo bambae hem i blesem yumi. Yumi wetem ol pris blong hem, hem bambae i blesem yumi.

13Hem bambae i blesem yumi evriwan we yumi stap ona long hem. Ol haeman wetem ol man we oli no gat nem, be hem bambae i mekem i sem mak nomo long yumi evriwan.

## Psalms 115:15 (id: 10019115015)

## Psalms 116:1 (id: 10019116001)

1Olgeta. Mi mi lavem Hae God tumas, from we oltaem hem i stap lesin long mi, oltaem hem i stap harem prea blong mi.


## Psalms 116:2 (id: 10019116002)

2Oltaem hem i stap lesin long mi, mo from samting ya, bambae mi stap prea long hem oltaem long laef blong mi.


## Psalms 116:3 (id: 10019116003)

3Bifo, bigfala trabol i kasem mi we klosap mi ded from. Mi mi luk ded i olsem wan trap we klosap i kasem mi, nao mi harem nogud tumas, mi fraet we mi fraet.


## Psalms 116:4 (id: 10019116004)

4Nao mi krae long hem, mi talem se, “Hae God. Plis yu sevem mi.”


## Psalms 116:5 (id: 10019116005)

5Hae God i God blong yumi, mo hem i gud tumas. Hem i kaen, mo i gat sore long man.


## Psalms 116:6 (id: 10019116006)

6Hem i stap lukaot long olgeta we oli no gat paoa. Taem trabol i kasem mi, hem i sevem mi.


## Psalms 116:7 (id: 10019116007)

7Mo naoia mi mi save harem gud bakegen, from we hem i stap mekem i gud tumas long mi.


## Psalms 116:8 (id: 10019116008)

## Psalms 116:9 (id: 10019116009)

12Hae God i stap mekem i gud tumas long mi. ?Bambae mi save givim wanem presen long hem?

## Psalms 116:14 (id: 10019116014)

## Psalms 116:16 (id: 10019116016)

1Olgeta. Ol man we laef blong olgeta i stret, we oltaem oli stap folem Loa\* blong Hae God, bambae oli save harem gud.


## Psalms 119:2 (id: 10019119002)

2Ol man we oli stap obei long hem long olgeta tingting blong olgeta, mo we oli stap folem ol rul we hem i mekem, bambae oli save harem gud.


## Psalms 119:3 (id: 10019119003)

3Neva oli mekem samting we i rong, oltaem oli stap folem fasin blong hem.


## Psalms 119:4 (id: 10019119004)

4Hae God. Yu yu givim ol loa blong yu long mi, blong mi mi holemstrong olgeta, mi obei long olgeta.


## Psalms 119:5 (id: 10019119005)

5Mi mi wantem tumas blong mi stap strong oltaem blong folem ol tijing blong yu.


## Psalms 119:6 (id: 10019119006)

6Sipos mi stap lesin gud long ol tok we yu yu putum, bambae ol man oli no save mekem mi mi sem.


## Psalms 119:7 (id: 10019119007)

7Ol tingting blong yu i stret, mo sipos mi stap lanem olgeta, bambae tingting blong mi i kam klin, nao bambae mi presemgud yu.


## Psalms 119:8 (id: 10019119008)

8Oltaem bambae mi obei long ol tijing blong yu. !Plis yu no lego mi!


## Psalms 119:9 (id: 10019119009)

9Hae God. Mi mi yangfala nomo. ?Bambae mi save mekem laef blong mi i klin olsem wanem? Mi mi mas obei long ol tok blong yu.


## Psalms 119:10 (id: 10019119010)

10Mi mi stap traehad long olgeta tingting blong mi, blong mekem wok blong yu. Plis yu halpem mi, blong mi no lego ol tok we yu yu putum.


## Psalms 119:11 (id: 10019119011)

11Oltaem mi stap holem ol tok blong yu long tingting blong mi, blong bambae mi no save mekem sin agens long yu.


## Psalms 119:12 (id: 10019119012)

12Hae God, mi mi presem yu. Plis yu tijim mi long ol rod blong yu.


## Psalms 119:13 (id: 10019119013)

13Bambae mi stap talemaot bakegen ol tingting blong yu we yu yu talem.


## Psalms 119:14 (id: 10019119014)

14Mi mi glad blong folem ol rul we yu yu mekem, i winim we mi mi glad from we mi rijman.


## Psalms 119:15 (id: 10019119015)

15Oltaem mi stap tingting long ol loa blong yu, mo mi stap tingbaot ol fasin blong yu.


## Psalms 119:16 (id: 10019119016)

16Oltaem mi mi glad tumas long ol tijing blong yu, mi no save fogetem ol tok blong yu.


## Psalms 119:17 (id: 10019119017)

17Hae God. Mi mi man blong wok blong yu. Plis yu mekem i gud long mi, nao bambae mi save stap laef, mi save obei long ol tok blong yu.


## Psalms 119:18 (id: 10019119018)

18Plis yu openem ae blong mi blong mi save luk ol gudgudfala samting long Loa\* blong yu.


## Psalms 119:19 (id: 10019119019)

19Bambae mi mi stap long wol ya blong smol taem nomo, plis yu no haedem ol tok ya long mi we yu yu putum.


## Psalms 119:20 (id: 10019119020)

20Oltaem nomo, mi mi wantem we mi wantem tumas blong savegud ol tingting blong yu.


## Psalms 119:21 (id: 10019119021)

21Ol praod man oli no save obei long ol tok we yu yu putum, nao yu tok long olgeta, yu talem long olgeta se oli mas lus.


## Psalms 119:22 (id: 10019119022)

22Olgeta ya oli stap sakem ol rabis tok long mi, mo oli stap laf nogud long mi. Plis yu sevem mi blong ol tok ya i no spolem mi, from we mi mi stap holem ol rul we yu yu mekem.


## Psalms 119:23 (id: 10019119023)

23Nating we ol haeman oli sidaon, oli mekem plan agens long mi, be mi mi man blong wok blong yu, mo oltaem bambae mi stap tingting long ol loa blong yu.


## Psalms 119:24 (id: 10019119024)

24Ol rul we yu yu mekem, oltaem oli stap mekem mi mi harem gud. Oli olsem man blong givim waestok long mi.


## Psalms 119:25 (id: 10019119025)

25Hae God. Mi harem se klosap mi mi ded. Plis yu mekem mi mi laef bakegen, olsem we tok blong yu i talem.


## Psalms 119:26 (id: 10019119026)

26Mi mi talemaot olgeta samting nogud we mi bin mekem, mo yu yu harem prea blong mi. Plis yu tijim mi long ol rod blong yu.


## Psalms 119:27 (id: 10019119027)

27Plis yu givhan long mi blong mi savegud ol loa blong yu, nao bambae mi stap tingting long ol bigbigfala wok blong yu.


## Psalms 119:28 (id: 10019119028)

28Mi mi harem nogud we i nogud. Plis yu mekem mi mi strong, olsem we tok blong yu i talem.


## Psalms 119:29 (id: 10019119029)

29Plis yu lukaot long mi blong mi no folem rod blong giaman, yu kaen long mi yu tijim mi long Loa\* blong yu.


## Psalms 119:30 (id: 10019119030)

30Mi mi jusumaot finis, se bambae mi holem ol fasin we yu yu wantem, mo bambae mi stap lesin long ol tingting blong yu.


## Psalms 119:31 (id: 10019119031)

31Hae God, mi mi stap folem ol rul we yu yu mekem. Plis yu no letem ol man oli mekem mi mi sem.


## Psalms 119:32 (id: 10019119032)

32Mi wantem tumas blong obei long ol tok we yu yu putum, from we yu bambae yu mekem mi mi save olgeta moa.


## Psalms 119:33 (id: 10019119033)

33Hae God. Plis yu lanem mi long mining blong ol tok we yu tijim mi long hem, nao bambae mi obei long hem oltaem.


## Psalms 119:34 (id: 10019119034)

34Yu talemaot mining blong Loa\* blong yu long mi, nao bambae mi obei long hem, mi holemgud long olgeta tingting blong mi.


## Psalms 119:35 (id: 10019119035)

35Plis yu givhan long mi blong oltaem mi stap obei long ol tok we yu yu putum, from we oli stap mekem mi mi glad.


## Psalms 119:36 (id: 10019119036)

36Plis yu mekem we oltaem bambae mi wantem moa blong obei long ol rul we yu yu mekem, i winim we mi wantem kam rijman.


## Psalms 119:37 (id: 10019119037)

37Plis yu blokem mi blong mi no save folem ol fasin ya we i nogat wan gudfala samting i save kamaot long hem. Plis yu mekem we mi mi gat gudfala laef, we i folemgud ol fasin blong yu.


## Psalms 119:38 (id: 10019119038)

38Mi mi man blong wok blong yu, plis yu holem promes blong yu we yu mekem long mi. Promes ya i stap long olgeta we oli stap obei long yu.


## Psalms 119:39 (id: 10019119039)

39Mi mi fraet tumas long ol tok nogud blong ol man we oli stap agens long mi. Plis yu sevem mi, from we ol tingting blong yu i gud tumas.


## Psalms 119:40 (id: 10019119040)

40Mi mi wantem tumas blong obei long ol loa blong yu. Plis yu mekem laef blong mi i kam niuwan, from we fasin blong yu i stret gud.


## Psalms 119:41 (id: 10019119041)

## Psalms 119:42 (id: 10019119042)

## Psalms 137:3 (id: 10019137003)

## Psalms 138:1 (id: 10019138001)

1Hae God. Mi mi talem tangkiu long yu long olgeta tingting blong mi. Mi stap sing blong presem yu long fes blong ol narafala god.


## Psalms 138:2 (id: 10019138002)

2Oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas, mo yu stap holem ol promes\* blong yu. Yu yu bin soemaot we nem blong yu mo ol tok we yu yu putum, tufala i hae we i hae, i winim ol narafala samting. From ol samting ya, oltaem mi stap lukluk i go long haos\* blong yu we i tabu, mi stap bodaon, mi stap presem nem blong yu.


## Psalms 138:3 (id: 10019138003)

3Taem mi mi krae i go long yu, yu yu harem krae blong mi, yu mekem mi mi strong moa long paoa blong yu.


## Psalms 138:4 (id: 10019138004)

4Hae God. Olgeta king long wol oli harem ol promes blong yu, mo bambae oli presem yu.


## Psalms 138:5 (id: 10019138005)

5Bambae oli singsing blong talemaot ol fasin blong yu, from we yu yu King, mo yu hae we yu hae.


## Psalms 138:6 (id: 10019138006)

6Be nating we yu yu hae olsem, oltaem yu stap lukaot gud long ol man we tingting blong olgeta i stap daon. Be ol praod man oli no save haed long fes blong yu.


## Psalms 138:7 (id: 10019138007)

7Taem ol trabol i stap kam long mi yu yu stap holem mi mi stap gud. Yu stap agens long ol enemi blong mi we oli stap kros tumas long mi, mo yu stap sevem mi long paoa blong yu.


## Psalms 138:8 (id: 10019138008)

8Hae God. Oltaem bambae yu yu lavem man gogo i no save finis. Bambae yu yu mekem evri samting we yu bin promes blong mekem long mi. Yu yu statem wok ya finis, plis yu gohed long hem gogo i finis.


## Psalms 139:1 (id: 10019139001)

1Hae God. Yu yu lukluk gud long mi finis, mo yu savegud mi.


## Psalms 139:2 (id: 10019139002)

2Yu yu save evri samting we mi stap mekem. Yu stap longwe, be yu save olgeta tingting blong mi.


## Psalms 139:3 (id: 10019139003)

3Nating we mi stap wok no mi stap spel, be yu yu stap luk mi, mo yu save olgeta samting we mi stap mekem.


## Psalms 139:4 (id: 10019139004)

4Nating we mi no tok yet, be yu yu save finis ol samting ya we bambae mi talem.


## Psalms 139:5 (id: 10019139005)

5Yu yu stap blokemgud mi long evri saed, yu stap lukaot gud long mi long paoa blong yu.


## Psalms 139:6 (id: 10019139006)

6Yu yu save mi we yu savegud mi, fasin blong yu blong save mi i dip we i dip, mi mi no naf blong save samting ya.


## Psalms 139:7 (id: 10019139007)

7?Weples bambae mi save go haed long hem blong yu no luk mi? ?Weples bambae mi save go long hem blong ronwe long yu?


## Psalms 139:8 (id: 10019139008)

8Sipos mi go long heven, be yu yu stap long ples ya, bambae yu luk mi, mo sipos mi go ledaon long Hom\* blong ol Dedman, be yu yu stap long ples ya tu, bambae yu faenem mi.


## Psalms 139:9 (id: 10019139009)

9Sipos mi ronwe, mi go long is, we mi go bitim ples ya we san i stap kam antap long hem, no mi go longwe long wes, mi go bitim ples ya we san i stap godaon long hem,


## Psalms 139:10 (id: 10019139010)

10be bambae yu yu stap long ples ya blong lidim mi, mo blong givhan long mi.


## Psalms 139:11 (id: 10019139011)

11Mi mi save askem tudak i kam blong mi haed long hem, mo mi save askem delaet we i stap raon long mi i kam naet,


## Psalms 139:12 (id: 10019139012)

12be tudak, hem i no dak nating long yu, mo naet, long yu hem i olsem dei nomo. Tudak mo delaet, long yu, tufala i sem mak nomo.


## Psalms 139:13 (id: 10019139013)

13Taem mi stap yet long bel blong mama blong mi, yu yu mekem evri haf blong bodi blong mi, mo yu mekem olgeta oli joen gud.


## Psalms 139:14 (id: 10019139014)

14Yu yu mekem mi long fasin we i narafala, mo i nambawan olgeta. Mi mi ona long yu, mo mi presem nem blong yu from. Olgeta samting we yu yu mekem oli gud we i gud tumas. Samting ya, mi mi save we mi savegud.


## Psalms 139:15 (id: 10019139015)

15Taem mi mi stap long bel blong mama blong mi, long ples ya we i haed we i haed, nao ol bun blong mi oli stap joen gud, mi stap gru, be yu yu luk mi we mi stap long ples ya.


## Psalms 139:16 (id: 10019139016)

16Taem mi mi no bon yet, be yu yu luk mi finis, mo olgeta dei we yu yu makemaot se oli blong mi, nating we oli no kamtru yet, be yu yu raetemdaon olgeta finis long buk blong yu.


## Psalms 139:17 (id: 10019139017)

17God, i strong tumas blong mi kasem ol tingting blong yu. Olgeta oli plante we plante.


## Psalms 139:18 (id: 10019139018)

18Sipos mi kaontem olgeta, bambae mi faenemaot se oli plante moa, i winim sanbij long solwota. Sipos mi mi slip, nao mi wekap bakegen, be bambae mi stap wetem yu yet.


## Psalms 139:19 (id: 10019139019)

19God. !Mi wantem tumas blong yu kilim ol man nogud ya oli ded! !Mi wantem tumas se ol man blong faet ya oli mas livim mi!


## Psalms 139:20 (id: 10019139020)

20Oli stap talem ol rabis tok agens long yu, oli stap tok nogud blong spolem nem blong yu.


## Psalms 139:21 (id: 10019139021)

21Hae God. Mi mi no laekem nating olgeta ya we oli no laekem yu, mi mi agens long olgeta from we oli stap agens long yu. Oltaem mi stap harem nogud from we olgeta ya oli stap mekem i nogud long yu.


## Psalms 139:22 (id: 10019139022)

22Mi mi no laekem olgeta we mi no laekem olgeta nating, oltaem mi stap makem olgeta, se oli enemi blong mi nomo.


## Psalms 139:23 (id: 10019139023)

23God, plis yu lukluk gud long mi, blong yu savegud ol tingting blong mi. Plis yu skelem laef blong mi, blong yu faenemaot ol samting we mi stap tingting long hem.


## Psalms 139:24 (id: 10019139024)

24Mo sipos i gat sam samting nogud long laef blong mi, plis i gud yu traem faenemaot, nao yu lidim mi long rod ya we i no save lus.


## Psalms 140:1 (id: 10019140001)

1Hae God. Plis yu tekemaot mi long han blong ol man nogud. Plis yu blokem ol man blong faet oli no kam long mi, mo yu lukaot gud long mi.


## Psalms 140:2 (id: 10019140002)

2Oltaem olgeta ya oli stap mekem plan blong mekem ol rabis fasin, mo oltaem oli stap traem blong statem rao.


## Psalms 140:3 (id: 10019140003)

3Maot blong olgeta i sap tumas, olsem tut blong rabis snek, mo ol tok blong olgeta i olsem posen blong snek.


## Psalms 140:4 (id: 10019140004)

4Hae God. Ol man nogud ya oli man blong faet, mo oli stap mekem plan blong mekem mi mi foldaon. Plis yu tekemaot mi long paoa blong olgeta, mo yu blokemgud mi.


## Psalms 140:5 (id: 10019140005)

5Ol praod man oli putum trap finis blong kasem mi, oli pulum ol net blong olgeta oli stap rere, mo oli taetem ol sling long rod blong mi, blong holem mi.


## Psalms 140:6-8 (id: 10019140006)

6\-8Hae God, yu yu Masta\* blong mi. Oltaem mi stap talem se, “Yu yu God blong mi.” Plis yu harem prea blong mi. Yu nao yu stap blokemgud mi long paoa blong yu, mo yu stap lukaot gud long mi long taem blong faet. Ol samting ya we ol man nogud oli wantem, plis yu no givim long olgeta. Plis yu no letem plan blong olgeta i kamtru olsem we oli wantem.


## Psalms 140:9 (id: 10019140009)

9Plis yu no letem ol enemi blong mi oli winim mi, yu mekem we ol strong tok blong olgeta i kasem olgeta bakegen.


## Psalms 140:10 (id: 10019140010)

10Mi wantem we faea i foldaon long olgeta, i bonem olgeta. Plis yu sakem olgeta i go insaed long wan hol we neva oli save kamaot bakegen.


## Psalms 140:11 (id: 10019140011)

11Mi wantem se ol man we oli stap mekem giaman toktok blong spolem nem blong narafala man, bambae oli no save win. Plis yu letem ol samting nogud i kasem ol man blong faet, i spolemgud olgeta wantaem nomo.


## Psalms 140:12 (id: 10019140012)

12Hae God. Mi save we yu yu stap givhan long ol puaman long ol trabol blong olgeta, mo yu stap mekem we olgeta oli kasem ol samting we oli gat raet blong kasem.


## Psalms 140:13 (id: 10019140013)

13I tru, ol stret man bambae oli stap wetem yu, mo oltaem bambae oli stap presem yu.


## Psalms 141:1 (id: 10019141001)

1Hae God. Mi mi stap krae i kam long yu. Plis yu kwik, yu givhan long mi. Mi stap krae i kam long yu, plis yu lesin long mi.


## Psalms 141:2 (id: 10019141002)

2Plis yu letem prea blong mi i kam long yu olsem smok blong insens\* we man i bonem i kam long yu. Taem mi leftemap han blong mi blong prea long yu, plis yu glad long mi olsem we yu glad long ol sakrefaes ya we pris\* i stap bonem long sapa.


## Psalms 141:3 (id: 10019141003)

3Hae God, plis yu blokem mi blong mi no stap talem ol rabis tok, Plis yu givhan long mi blong mi no save tok nogud long ol man.


## Psalms 141:4 (id: 10019141004)

4Plis yu lukaot long mi blong mi no mekem ol samting we i rong, mo blong mi no go joen wetem ol man nogud long ol rabis fasin blong olgeta. Mi wantem se neva bambae mi go joen long ol lafet blong olgeta.


## Psalms 141:5 (id: 10019141005)

5Mi mi save letem gudfala man i kilim mi no i tok strong long mi, blong stretem mi. Be ol man nogud, oltaem mi stap prea blong yu blokem ol rabis fasin blong olgeta, mo from samting ya, mi no save letem olgeta oli leftemap nem blong mi.


## Psalms 141:6 (id: 10019141006)

6Nao taem yu sakem ol haeman blong olgeta oli godaon long klif, bambae ol man oli luksave se ol tok blong mi i tru.


## Psalms 141:7 (id: 10019141007)

7Bambae yu sakem ol bun blong olgeta olbaot long beregraon, olsem wud we man i splitim, i katkatem long ol smosmol pis.


## Psalms 141:8 (id: 10019141008)

8Hae God, yu yu God blong mi, mo mi mi stap trastem yu oltaem. Mi wantem tumas blong yu yu stap lukaot gud long mi. Plis yu no letem mi mi ded.


## Psalms 141:9 (id: 10019141009)

9Plis yu blokem mi blong mi no foldaon long ol hol we ol man nogud oli digim blong kasem mi, mo blong mi no fas long ol sling we olgeta oli putum.


## Psalms 141:10 (id: 10019141010)

10Plis yu letem olgeta oli foldaon bakegen long ol trap blong olgeta, be yu letem mi mi wokbaot i go, we i no gat wan samting i spolem mi.


## Psalms 142:1 (id: 10019142001)

1Olgeta. Mi mi krae i go long Hae God blong hem i givhan long mi. Mi stap askem long hem blong hem i sore long mi.


## Psalms 142:2 (id: 10019142002)

2Mi talemaot olgeta samting long hem we i mekem mi mi harem nogud, mi talemaot olgeta trabol blong mi long hem.


## Psalms 142:3 (id: 10019142003)

3Taem mi mi harem nogud tumas, we tingting blong mi i foldaon from, be hem i save wanem samting we bambae mi mas mekem. Long rod ya we mi stap wokbaot long hem, ol enemi blong mi oli putum wan trap finis blong kasem mi.


## Psalms 142:4 (id: 10019142004)

4Be taem mi luk biaen long mi, mi no luk wan man we i save givhan long mi, i no gat man we i save blokemgud mi, i no gat wan man nating we i save lukaot gud long mi.


## Psalms 142:5 (id: 10019142005)

5Hae God, mi stap krae long yu blong yu givhan long mi. Mi talem se, “Yu nomo yu stap lukaot long mi. Olgeta laef blong mi long wol ya, yu nao.”


## Psalms 142:6 (id: 10019142006)

6Tingting blong mi i foldaon olgeta. Plis yu lesin long mi we mi stap krae long yu blong yu givhan long mi. Ol enemi ya blong mi oli strong tumas winim mi, plis yu tekemaot mi long han blong olgeta.


## Psalms 142:7 (id: 10019142007)

7Mi mi stap long trabol ya olsem we mi stap long kalabus. Plis yu mekem mi mi fri, nao bambae mi presem yu long fes blong ol man blong yu, from we yu yu mekem i gud tumas long mi.


## Psalms 143:1 (id: 10019143001)

1Hae God. Plis yu harem prea blong mi. Fasin blong yu i stret, mo oltaem yu stap holem promes blong yu we yu mekem wetem man. Plis yu lesin long tok blong mi, yu harem krae blong mi.


## Psalms 143:2 (id: 10019143002)

2Mi mi man blong wok blong yu, be yu yu save, long wol ya, i no gat wan man we i stret, mifala evriwan i stap mekem i nogud. Plis yu no jajem mi.


## Psalms 143:3 (id: 10019143003)

3Enemi ya blong mi i stap ronem mi gogo i kasem mi, nao hem i flatem mi, i winimgud mi. Hem i putum mi long kalabus ya we i tudak olgeta, nao mi mi stap olsem man we i ded longtaem finis.


## Psalms 143:4 (id: 10019143004)

4Nao mi mi harem nogud, mi wantem livim evri samting, from we tingting blong mi i foldaon we i foldaon olgeta.


## Psalms 143:5 (id: 10019143005)

5Be taem mi stap tingtingbak long ol dei ya we i pas finis, mi stap tingbaot olgeta wok ya we yu yu mekem, mo mi stap tingting long olgeta samting ya we yu yu mekem long mi.


## Psalms 143:6 (id: 10019143006)

6Naoia laef blong mi i olsem graon we i drae we i drae. Mi mi wantem yu tumas, olsem man we i tosta i wantem tumas blong dring wota, nao mi stap leftemap han blong mi blong prea long yu.


## Psalms 143:7 (id: 10019143007)

7!Hae God, plis yu kwik! !Plis yu harem krae blong mi! Tingting blong mi i foldaon olgeta. !Plis yu no haedem fes blong yu long mi! I nogud mi go wetem olgeta we oli godaon long Hom\* blong ol Dedman.


## Psalms 143:8 (id: 10019143008)

8Mi mi putum tingting blong mi i stap strong long yu. Evri moning, plis yu soemaot long mi bakegen we oltaem yu yu lavem man tumas. Oltaem prea blong mi i stap go antap long yu. Plis yu soemaot rod long mi, blong mi mi folem.


## Psalms 143:9 (id: 10019143009)

9Hae God, mi mi kam long yu blong yu blokemgud mi, yu lukaot gud long mi. Plis yu tekemaot mi long han blong ol enemi blong mi.


## Psalms 143:10 (id: 10019143010)

10Yu yu God blong mi, plis yu tijim mi blong mi mekem ol samting we yu yu wantem. Yu yu gud tumas long mi, plis yu lidim mi long rod we i sef.


## Psalms 143:11 (id: 10019143011)

11Hae God, fasin blong yu i gud tumas. Plis yu sevem mi olsem we yu bin promes, yu tekemaot mi long ol trabol ya we mi stap long hem.


## Psalms 143:12 (id: 10019143012)

12Mi mi man blong wok blong yu, we oltaem yu lavem mi tumas. Plis yu kilim olgeta enemi ya blong mi, yu spolem olgeta ya we oli stap spolem mi.


## Psalms 144:1 (id: 10019144001)

1Olgeta. Mi mi presem Hae God we i stap lukaot long mi. Hem i stap tijim mi blong mekem mi mi rere blong faet.


## Psalms 144:2 (id: 10019144002)

2Hem i stap blokemgud mi, mo i stap lukaot gud long mi. Hem i sefples blong mi mo i Sevia blong mi. Mi mi stap trastem hem nomo blong i sevem mi. Hem nao i stap mekem ol man blong ol narafala kantri oli stap aninit long paoa blong mi.


## Psalms 144:3 (id: 10019144003)

3Hae God. Mi mi luk man se hem i samting nating nomo. I no stret blong yu yu lukluk long hem. I no stret nating blong yu yu tingbaot hem.


## Psalms 144:4 (id: 10019144004)

4Hem i olsem win we i blu smoltaem nomo nao i finis. Ol dei blong hem i olsem wan sado we i pas finis.


## Psalms 144:5 (id: 10019144005)

5Hae God. Plis yu openem skae yu kamdaon, yu tajem ol hil long han blong yu, nao bambae smok i kamaot long olgeta.


## Psalms 144:6 (id: 10019144006)

6Yu sanem laetning i kam, i mekem ol enemi blong yu oli ron wanwan yu sutum olgeta long ol ara blong yu, blong mekem olgeta oli ronwe.


## Psalms 144:7 (id: 10019144007)

7Plis yu pusum han blong yu i kam, yu pulumaot mi long dip wota ya, yu sevem mi. Yu tekemaot mi long paoa blong ol man ya blong narafala kantri,


## Psalms 144:8 (id: 10019144008)

8we neva oli talem trutok. Olgeta ya, nating we oli stap talem nem blong yu blong mekem tok blong olgeta i strong, be oli stap giaman nomo.


## Psalms 144:9 (id: 10019144009)

9God, bambae mi singsing long yu long wan niufala sing, bambae mi kilim gita blong mi, mi sing long yu.


## Psalms 144:10 (id: 10019144010)

10Oltaem yu stap mekem ol king oli win long faet. Mi mi man blong wok blong yu, mo yu yu stap sevem mi.


## Psalms 144:11 (id: 10019144011)

11Ol enemi blong mi, fasin blong olgeta i strong tumas. Plis yu blokem olgeta oli no kam spolem mi yu sevem mi. Yu tekemaot mi long paoa blong ol man ya blong narafala kantri, we neva oli talem trutok. Olgeta ya, nating we oli stap talem nem blong yu blong mekem tok blong olgeta i strong, be oli stap giaman nomo.


## Psalms 144:12 (id: 10019144012)

12Mifala i wantem se ol yang boe blong mifala bambae oli kam strong olsem ol yang tri we oli stap gru gud, mo ol dota blong mifala bambae oli naes olsem ol pos blong kona blong haos blong king we oli katem blong flasem haos ya.


## Psalms 144:13 (id: 10019144013)

13Mifala i wantem se ol garen blong mifala oli karem plante kakae, nao ol haos blong kakae blong mifala bambae oli fulap gud, mo ol sipsip long ol fenis blong mifala bambae oli karem pikinini we i plante we plante.


## Psalms 144:14 (id: 10019144014)

14Mifala i wantem se ol buluk blong mifala bambae oli no save lusum ol pikinini blong olgeta blong oli save kam plante we plante. Mifala i wantem se ol enemi blong mifala bambae oli no moa stap klosap long mifala, nao ol man blong mifala bambae oli no moa save krae olbaot long ples blong mifala, bambae oli save harem gud.


## Psalms 144:15 (id: 10019144015)

15Hae God i God blong yumi, mo from samting ya, yumi save harem gud. Mo sipos hem i mekem ol samting ya i kamtru long kantri ya blong yumi, bambae yumi glad we yumi glad.


## Psalms 145:1 (id: 10019145001)

1Hae God. Yu yu God blong mi, mo yu yu King blong mi. Oltaem bambae mi stap talemaot hae nem blong yu, bambae mi stap talem tangkiu long yu, gogo i no save finis.


## Psalms 145:2 (id: 10019145002)

2Evri dei, bambae mi stap talem tangkiu long yu, bambae mi stap presem yu oltaem, gogo i no save finis.


## Psalms 145:3 (id: 10019145003)

3Hae God, hem i hae we i hae, mo i stret blong ol man oli stap leftemap nem blong hem i go antap. Hem i hae we i hae, man i no naf blong save paoa blong hem.


## Psalms 145:4 (id: 10019145004)

4Hae God. Oltaem ol man bambae oli stap tokbaot ol wok blong yu long fes blong ol pikinini we bambae oli bon biaen long olgeta. Oltaem bambae oli stap talemaot ol bigbigfala wok blong yu.


## Psalms 145:5 (id: 10019145005)

5Yu yu King, mo oltaem bambae oli stap tokbaot paoa blong yu mo hae nem blong yu, mo oltaem bambae mi stap tingting gud long ol gudgudfala wok blong yu.


## Psalms 145:6 (id: 10019145006)

6Ol man bambae oli stap tokbaot ol bigbigfala wok ya blong yu, mo mi bambae mi stap talemaot we yu yu hae we yu hae.


## Psalms 145:7 (id: 10019145007)

7Bambae oli stap tokbaot olgeta gudgudfala fasin blong yu, mo bambae oli stap singsing blong talemaot we yu yu kaen tumas.


## Psalms 145:8 (id: 10019145008)

8Hae God, hem i God we i lavem yumi, mo i gat sore long yumi. Hem i no save kros kwik, oltaem hem i stap lavem yumi.


## Psalms 145:9 (id: 10019145009)

9Hem i mekem olgeta samting we i gat laef, mo oltaem hem i gat sore long olgeta. Oltaem hem i stap mekem i gud tumas long yumi evriwan.


## Psalms 145:10 (id: 10019145010)

10Hae God. Olgeta samting ya we yu yu mekem, bambae oli presem yu, mo olgeta man blong yu bambae oli talem tangkiu long yu.


## Psalms 145:11 (id: 10019145011)

11Yu yu King, mo bambae oli stap tokbaot ol gudgudfala samting we yu yu mekem long paoa blong yu, mo bambae oli stap talemaot bigfala paoa we yu yu gat,


## Psalms 145:12 (id: 10019145012)

12blong olgeta man bambae oli save ol bigbigfala wok blong yu, mo ol gudgudfala samting blong hae nem blong yu.


## Psalms 145:13 (id: 10019145013)

13Yu nomo yu King mo bambae yu yu stap rul oltaem, gogo i no save finis. Oltaem Hae God i stap holem ol promes blong hem, mo evri samting we hem i stap mekem oli gud tumas.


## Psalms 145:14 (id: 10019145014)

14Hem i stap givhan long olgeta we oli stap long trabol, mo i stap leftemap olgeta we oli foldaon.


## Psalms 145:15 (id: 10019145015)

15Hae God. Olgeta samting we yu yu mekem, oli stap lukluk long yu, mo taem olgeta oli hanggri, yu yu stap givim kakae long olgeta.


## Psalms 145:16 (id: 10019145016)

16Yu stap givim plante kakae long olgeta, mo yu stap mekem olgeta evriwan oli harem gud.


## Psalms 145:17 (id: 10019145017)

17Fasin blong Hae God i stret gud, mo olgeta samting we hem i mekem i stap soemaot samting ya. Hem i God we i gat sore long man, mo olgeta wok blong hem i stap soemaot fasin ya blong hem.


## Psalms 145:18 (id: 10019145018)

18Oltaem hem i stap klosap long olgeta we oli stap krae i go long hem, we hemia olgeta ya we oli wantem tru blong hem i givhan long olgeta.


## Psalms 145:19 (id: 10019145019)

19Olgeta we oli stap ona long hem, oltaem hem i stap givim ol samting long olgeta we oli sot long hem, oltaem hem i stap harem krae blong olgeta, mo i stap sevem olgeta.


## Psalms 145:20 (id: 10019145020)

20Oltaem hem i stap lukaot gud long olgeta we oli lavem hem, be bambae hem i spolem olgeta we oli stap mekem ol fasin nogud.


## Psalms 145:21 (id: 10019145021)

21Oltaem bambae mi mi presem Hae God. Nem blong hem i tabu, mo i gud olgeta samting we hem i mekem oli stap presem hem oltaem, gogo i no save finis.


## Psalms 146:1 (id: 10019146001)

1Olgeta. !Yumi mas presem Hae God! !Mi mi mas presem hem!


## Psalms 146:2 (id: 10019146002)

2Oltaem long laef blong mi, bambae mi stap presem hem. Hem i God blong mi, mo bambae mi stap sing blong presem hem.


## Psalms 146:3 (id: 10019146003)

3Yufala i no mas trastem ol lida blong wol ya. Olgeta oli no save sevem yufala.


## Psalms 146:4 (id: 10019146004)

4Taem oli ded, oli stap gobak long graon nomo, nao ol plan blong olgeta oli no save kamtru.


## Psalms 146:5-6 (id: 10019146005)

5\-6Hae God i God blong Jekob.\* Hem i mekem skae, mo graon, mo solwota, wetem olgeta samting we i stap long trifala, mo man we i putum tingting blong hem i stap long hem, hem bambae i harem gud. Oltaem Hae God i stap holem ol promes blong hem,


## Psalms 146:7 (id: 10019146007)

7oltaem hem i stap givhan long olgeta we ol narafala man oli stap mekem i no stret long olgeta, oli stap spolem olgeta, mo oltaem hem i stap givim kakae long olgeta we oli hanggri. Hem i stap mekem ol man we oli stap long kalabus oli go fri,


## Psalms 146:8 (id: 10019146008)

8mo i stap openem ae blong ol blaenman. Oltaem hem i stap leftemap olgeta we oli foldaon. Ol man we fasin blong olgeta i stret, hem i stap lavem olgeta.


## Psalms 146:9 (id: 10019146009)

9Hem i stap lukaot gud long ol strenja we oli stap long ples blong yumi, mo i stap givhan long ol wido, mo ol pikinini we papa blong olgeta i ded, be ol man nogud, hem i stap spolem ol plan blong olgeta.


## Psalms 146:10 (id: 10019146010)

10Hae God, hem bambae i King oltaem, gogo i no save finis. Yumi ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* God ya blong yumi bambae i rul oltaem nomo, gogo i no save finis. !Oltaem yumi mas presem Hae God!


## Psalms 147:1 (id: 10019147001)

1Olgeta. !Yumi mas presem Hae God! Hem i God blong yumi, mo i gud yumi singsing blong presem hem, from we fasin ya i gud tumas, mo i stret tu.


## Psalms 147:2 (id: 10019147002)

2Hae God i stap stanemap Jerusalem\* bakegen, mo i stap tekem ol man blong yumi we oli stap wok slef\* long ol narafala kantri, oli kambak long ples blong yumi.


## Psalms 147:3 (id: 10019147003)

3Hem i stap mekem olgeta we oli harem nogud, oli harem gud bakegen, mo i stap fasem ol soa blong olgeta.


## Psalms 147:4-6 (id: 10019147004)

4\-6Hae God i Masta blong yumi, mo hem i hae we i hae. Hem i makem namba blong ol sta, mo i putum nem blong olgeta wanwan. Hem i gat olgeta paoa, mo man i no save makem waes blong hem. Hem i stap leftemap olgeta we tingting blong olgeta i stap daon, be i stap flatemgud ol man nogud i godaon long graon.


## Psalms 147:7 (id: 10019147007)

7Yumi mas sing long ol singsing blong presem hem, yumi mas sing wetem ol gita blong yumi blong talem tangkiu long hem.


## Psalms 147:8 (id: 10019147008)

8Hem i stap putum ol klaod long skae, mo i stap sanem ren i kam long graon, mo i stap mekem gras i gru long ol hil.


## Psalms 147:9 (id: 10019147009)

9Hem i stap givim kakae long ol anamol, mo taem ol pikinini blong pijin oli krae, hem i stap fidim olgeta.


## Psalms 147:10 (id: 10019147010)

10Nating we ol hos oli strong blong mekem wok, mo ol soldia oli no save fraet nating, be Hae God i no glad long olgeta,


## Psalms 147:11 (id: 10019147011)

11hem i glad moa long ol man we oli stap ona long hem, mo long olgeta we oli stap trastem hem, se oltaem hem i lavem man tumas.


## Psalms 147:12 (id: 10019147012)

12Hae God i God blong yumi. !Yumi ol man Saeon,\* yumi mas leftemap nem blong hem! !Yumi mas presem hem!


## Psalms 147:13 (id: 10019147013)

13Hem i stap mekem ol doa\* blong stonwol blong yumi i strong we i strong, mo i stap blesem yumi evriwan.


## Psalms 147:14 (id: 10019147014)

14Hem i stap mekem kantri blong yumi i stap kwaet, mo i stap givim ol gudgudfala kakae long yumi blong i mekem yumi harem gud.


## Psalms 147:15 (id: 10019147015)

15Taem hem i givim tok, wantaem nomo wol ya i obei long hem.


## Psalms 147:16 (id: 10019147016)

16Long koltaem, hem i stap mekem wota blong naet i kam aes, mo i stap mekem sno i kam kavremap olgeta ples olsem blangket.


## Psalms 147:17 (id: 10019147017)

17Hem i stap sanem ston blong aes\* i kam plante olsem korel, nao i no gat man i save stap gud long koltaem ya we hem i stap sanem i kam.


## Psalms 147:18 (id: 10019147018)

18Be taem hem i tok, win i blu, nao ol aes ya i ronaot, i kam wota, mo ol reva oli stap ron bakegen.


## Psalms 147:19 (id: 10019147019)

19Yumi ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* mo oltaem Hae God i stap givim tok blong hem long yumi, i stap tijim yumi long ol rod blong hem, mo long ol tingting blong hem.


## Psalms 147:20 (id: 10019147020)

20Hem i no mekem samting ya long ol man blong ol narafala kantri. Olgeta ya oli no save nating ol tingting blong hem. !Oltaem yumi mas presem Hae God!


## Psalms 148:1 (id: 10019148001)

1Olgeta. !Yumi mas presem Hae God! Yufala evriwan we i stap long heven yufala i mas presem Hae God.


## Psalms 148:2 (id: 10019148002)

2Yufala ol enjel blong hem long heven, yufala i soldia blong hem, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:3 (id: 10019148003)

3San mo mun, yutufala i mas presem hem. Yufala ol sta, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:4 (id: 10019148004)

4Skae, yu we yu stap antap olgeta, yu mas presem hem, mo wota, yu we yu stap antap biaen long skae ya, yu mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:5 (id: 10019148005)

5I gud olgeta ya evriwan oli presem nem blong Hae God, from we hem i mekem olgeta long tok blong hem.


## Psalms 148:6 (id: 10019148006)

6Hem i putum loa long ol samting ya, se oli mas stap olwe long ples blong olgeta, mo loa ya bambae i no save lus.


## Psalms 148:7 (id: 10019148007)

7Yufala ol samting we i stap long wol ya, yufala i mas presem Hae God. Yufala ol bigbigfala fis, mo dipsi, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:8 (id: 10019148008)

8Laetning, wetem ol ston blong aes,\* sno, wetem ol klaod, yufala i mas presem hem. Hariken, yu yu stap obei long hem, yu mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:9 (id: 10019148009)

9Yufala ol bigbigfala hil wetem ol smosmol hil, yufala ol tri blong kakae wetem ol narafala tri, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:10 (id: 10019148010)

10Yufala ol anamol blong vilej wetem ol anamol blong bus, yufala ol snek wetem ol pijin, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:11 (id: 10019148011)

11Yufala ol king wetem ol man blong evri ples, yufala ol haeman wetem ol lida, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:12 (id: 10019148012)

12Yufala ol gel wetem ol boe, yufala ol ololfala wetem ol pikinini tu, yufala i mas presem hem.


## Psalms 148:13 (id: 10019148013)

13Yumi evriwan i mas presem nem blong Hae God. Nem blong hem i hae moa, i winim ol narafala nem, mo paoa blong hem i kasem evri ples long heven mo long wol.


## Psalms 148:14 (id: 10019148014)

14Yumi ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* yumi ol man blong hem, mo yumi stap klosap moa long hem. Hem nao i mekem yumi kam strong blong yumi stap presem hem oltaem. !Oltaem yumi mas presem Hae God!


## Psalms 149:1 (id: 10019149001)

1Olgeta. !Yumi presem Hae God! Yumi ya, yumi ol man blong hem. I gud yumi wokem wan niufala singsing, yumi kam wanples long fes blong ol narafala man blong hem, yumi sing long hem blong presem hem.


## Psalms 149:2 (id: 10019149002)

2Yumi ol laen\* blong Isrel,\* hem i mekem yumi. I gud yumi joen long hem, yumi glad. Yumi ol man Saeon,\* hem i King blong yumi. I gud yumi joen long hem, yumi glad.


## Psalms 149:3 (id: 10019149003)

3I gud yumi danis blong presem nem blong hem, yumi kilim ol dram mo ol gita blong yumi blong presem hem.


## Psalms 149:4 (id: 10019149004)

4Oltaem Hae God i stap glad long yumi. Oltaem tingting blong yumi i stap daon, nao hem i mekem yumi win.


## Psalms 149:5 (id: 10019149005)

5Yumi ol man blong hem, i gud yumi glad from we yumi win, i gud yumi singsing delaet from we yumi glad.


## Psalms 149:6 (id: 10019149006)

6I gud yumi holem ol naef blong faet blong yumi we oli sap tu saed, nao yumi presem hem we yumi stap singsingaot bigwan.


## Psalms 149:7 (id: 10019149007)

7Bambae yumi go faet long ol narafala kantri we bambae yumi winim olgeta, yumi panisim ol man blong olgeta.


## Psalms 149:8 (id: 10019149008)

8Bambae yumi putum hankaf long ol king blong olgeta, mo bambae yumi fasem ol lida blong olgeta long ol bigbigfala jen.


## Psalms 149:9 (id: 10019149009)

9Long fasin ya, bambae yumi panisim ol man ya olsem we Hae God i talem. Hemia fasin we hem i stap folem blong leftemap nem blong yumi. !Oltaem yumi mas presem Hae God!


## Psalms 150:1 (id: 10019150001)

1Olgeta. !Yumi mas presem Hae God! Yumi mas presem hem long haos\* blong hem. Yufala long heven, yufala i mas presem hem from bigfala paoa blong hem.


## Psalms 150:2 (id: 10019150002)

2Yumi mas presem hem from ol bigbigfala wok we hem i mekem. Nem blong hem i hae we i hae, mo yumi mas presem hem.


## Psalms 150:3 (id: 10019150003)

3Yumi mas presem hem long pupu. Yumi mas presem hem long gita, mo long mandolin.


## Psalms 150:4 (id: 10019150004)

4Yumi mas presem hem long dram, mo long danis. Yumi mas presem hem long yukalele, mo long bambu paep.


## Psalms 150:5 (id: 10019150005)

5Yumi mas presem hem long simbol.\* Yumi mas presem hem long simbol ya we i singaot bigwan.


## Psalms 150:6 (id: 10019150006)

6Yumi olgeta samting we yumi gat laef, yumi evriwan i mas presem hem. !Oltaem yumi mas presem Hae God!
